===== app/assets/fichiers_internes/aid_calculator_service.rb =====

# app/services/aid_calculator_service.rb
#
# Calcule les aides éligibles pour une propriété donnée
# en s'appuyant sur la table aid_rules (règles traçables et modifiables).
#
# Usage :
#   result = AidCalculatorService.new(property).call
#   result[:total]   # => 24500.0
#   result[:aids]    # => [{ name: "MPR parcours accompagné", amount: 12500, ... }, ...]
#   result[:errors]  # => ["Revenus non renseignés — MPR non calculable"]

class AidCalculatorService

  CEE_BY_WORK = {
    "ite"      => 565,
    "iti"      => 320,
    "sarking"  => 533,
    "vmc"      => 275,
    "menuiseries" => 0   # variable, non intégré pour l'instant
  }.freeze

  GRAND_NANCY_PER_M2 = {
    "ite"      => 30,
    "iti"      => 10,
    "sarking"  => 50
  }.freeze

  MPR_TAUX = {
    "tres_modeste"  => 0.70,
    "modeste"       => 0.50,
    "intermediaire" => 0.35,
    "superieur"     => 0.15
  }.freeze

  def initialize(property)
    @p      = property
    @aids   = []
    @errors = []
  end

  def call
    calculate_mpr
    calculate_cee
    calculate_eco_ptz
    calculate_grand_nancy_dernier_geste
    calculate_grand_nancy_bonus_bbc

    {
      aids:       @aids,
      total:      @aids.sum { |a| a[:amount] },
      errors:     @errors,
      calculated_at: Time.current
    }
  end

  private

  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  # 1. MaPrimeRénov' — Parcours accompagné
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  def calculate_mpr
    rule = AidRule.find_by(slug: "mpr_parcours_accompagne", active: true)
    return unless rule

    unless @p.income_bracket.present?
      @errors << "Revenus non renseignés — MaPrimeRénov' non calculable"
      return
    end

    unless eligible_parcours_accompagne?
      @errors << "Non éligible parcours accompagné (vérifier : 2 gestes isolation, ventilation, saut 2 classes DPE)"
      return
    end

    taux = MPR_TAUX[@p.income_bracket] || 0
    base_ht = estimated_cost_ht
    montant = (base_ht * taux).round

    # Bonus sortie passoire (+10%) si DPE G ou F → D ou mieux
    if sortie_passoire?
      bonus = (base_ht * 0.10).round
      montant += bonus
      note = " + 10% bonus sortie passoire"
    end

    montant = [montant, rule.amount_max.to_f].min

    @aids << {
      slug:        rule.slug,
      name:        rule.name,
      type:        "mpr",
      amount:      montant,
      basis:       "#{(taux * 100).to_i}% du coût HT estimé#{note}",
      source:      rule.source_label,
      valid_until: rule.valid_until,
      confidence:  :medium  # dépend du coût réel des travaux
    }
  end

  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  # 2. CEE — par poste de travaux
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  def calculate_cee
    rule = AidRule.find_by(slug: "cee_isolation", active: true)
    return unless rule

    total_cee = 0
    detail = []

    travaux_prevus.each do |travail|
      montant = CEE_BY_WORK[travail]
      next unless montant && montant > 0
      total_cee += montant
      detail << "#{travail.upcase} : #{montant} €"
    end

    return if total_cee == 0

    @aids << {
      slug:        rule.slug,
      name:        rule.name,
      type:        "cee",
      amount:      total_cee,
      basis:       detail.join(" | "),
      source:      rule.source_label,
      valid_until: rule.valid_until,
      confidence:  :low  # très variable selon l'offre de l'artisan RGE
    }
  end

  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  # 3. Éco-PTZ
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  def calculate_eco_ptz
    rule = AidRule.find_by(slug: "eco_ptz", active: true)
    return unless rule

    nb_gestes = travaux_prevus.size
    return if nb_gestes < 3

    montant = eligible_parcours_accompagne? ? 50_000 : 30_000

    @aids << {
      slug:        rule.slug,
      name:        rule.name,
      type:        "eco_ptz",
      amount:      montant,
      basis:       "#{nb_gestes} gestes — prêt à taux zéro#{eligible_parcours_accompagne? ? ' (parcours accompagné)' : ''}",
      source:      rule.source_label,
      valid_until: rule.valid_until,
      confidence:  :high,
      note:        "Remboursable à la vente du bien. Durée max 15 ans."
    }
  end

  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  # 4. Grand Nancy — Prime dernier geste
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  def calculate_grand_nancy_dernier_geste
    return unless territory_grand_nancy?
    rule = AidRule.find_by(slug: "grand_nancy_dernier_geste", active: true)
    return unless rule

    # Conditions : DPE cible C ou mieux, déclaré comme dernier geste
    dpe_cible = @p.dpe_target&.upcase
    return unless %w[A B C].include?(dpe_cible)

    total = 0
    detail = []

    if surface_ite > 0
      montant = surface_ite * GRAND_NANCY_PER_M2["ite"]
      total += montant
      detail << "ITE #{surface_ite}m² × 30€ = #{montant}€"
    end
    if surface_iti > 0
      montant = surface_iti * GRAND_NANCY_PER_M2["iti"]
      total += montant
      detail << "ITI #{surface_iti}m² × 10€ = #{montant}€"
    end
    if surface_sarking > 0
      montant = surface_sarking * GRAND_NANCY_PER_M2["sarking"]
      total += montant
      detail << "Sarking #{surface_sarking}m² × 50€ = #{montant}€"
    end

    return if total == 0

    @aids << {
      slug:        rule.slug,
      name:        rule.name,
      type:        "local",
      amount:      total,
      basis:       detail.join(" | "),
      source:      rule.source_label,
      valid_until: rule.valid_until,
      confidence:  :medium,
      note:        "Nécessite un audit prouvant que c'est le dernier geste et l'atteinte de l'étiquette C."
    }
  end

  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  # 5. Grand Nancy — Bonus BBC
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  def calculate_grand_nancy_bonus_bbc
    return unless territory_grand_nancy?
    rule = AidRule.find_by(slug: "grand_nancy_bonus_bbc", active: true)
    return unless rule

    dpe_cible = @p.dpe_target&.upcase
    return unless %w[A B].include?(dpe_cible)
    return unless eligible_parcours_accompagne?

    taux = MPR_TAUX[@p.income_bracket] || 0
    base_ht = estimated_cost_ht
    bonus = (base_ht * 0.05).round

    @aids << {
      slug:        rule.slug,
      name:        rule.name,
      type:        "local",
      amount:      bonus,
      basis:       "+5% sur le taux MPR (#{(taux * 100).to_i}% → #{((taux + 0.05) * 100).to_i}%)",
      source:      rule.source_label,
      valid_until: rule.valid_until,
      confidence:  :medium,
      note:        "Non cumulable avec la prime dernier geste."
    }
  end

  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  # Helpers
  # ----------------------------------------------------------

  def eligible_parcours_accompagne?
    nb_gestes = travaux_prevus.count { |t| %w[ite iti sarking menuiseries].include?(t) }
    ventilation = travaux_prevus.include?("vmc")
    dpe_actuel  = @p.dpe_class&.upcase
    dpe_cible   = @p.dpe_target&.upcase
    saut        = dpe_saut(dpe_actuel, dpe_cible)

    nb_gestes >= 2 && ventilation && saut >= 2
  end

  def sortie_passoire?
    dpe_actuel = @p.dpe_class&.upcase
    dpe_cible  = @p.dpe_target&.upcase
    %w[F G].include?(dpe_actuel) && %w[A B C D].include?(dpe_cible)
  end

  def dpe_saut(actuel, cible)
    ordre = %w[A B C D E F G]
    return 0 unless actuel && cible
    (ordre.index(actuel) || 0) - (ordre.index(cible) || 0)
  end

  def territory_grand_nancy?
    # Basé sur le code postal — les communes du Grand Nancy
    cp = @p.zipcode.to_s
    grand_nancy_zipcodes = %w[54000 54100 54200 54300 54500 54510 54520 54600 54700 54710 54140 54180 54230 54340 54400]
    grand_nancy_zipcodes.include?(cp)
  end

  def travaux_prevus
    # À terme : liste structurée sur la propriété
    # Pour l'instant : déduit du DPE et de la description
    travaux = []
    dpe = @p.dpe_class&.upcase
    desc = @p.description.to_s.downcase

    travaux << "ite"      if desc.include?("ite") || desc.include?("extérieur") || surface_ite > 0
    travaux << "iti"      if desc.include?("iti") || desc.include?("intérieur") || surface_iti > 0
    travaux << "sarking"  if desc.include?("sarking") || desc.include?("toiture") || surface_sarking > 0
    travaux << "vmc"      if desc.include?("vmc") || desc.include?("ventilation")
    travaux << "menuiseries" if desc.include?("fenêtre") || desc.include?("menuiserie")

    # Fallback : si DPE F/G et aucun travaux détecté, supposer ITE + VMC
    if travaux.empty? && %w[F G].include?(dpe)
      travaux = ["ite", "vmc"]
    end

    travaux.uniq
  end

  def estimated_cost_ht
    # Priorité : simulation existante, sinon estimation grossière
    if @p.device_simulation&.total_aid_estimate
      (@p.device_simulation.total_aid_estimate * 1.8 / 1.10).round  # approximation HT
    else
      # Estimation par surface : ~600€/m² pour réno globale
      (@p.surface.to_f * 600 / 1.10).round
    end
  end

  # Surfaces par poste — à terme champs dédiés sur Property
  # Pour l'instant : valeurs ALEC sur 3 impasse du Canal comme référence
  def surface_ite;     @p.respond_to?(:surface_ite)     ? @p.surface_ite.to_f     : 45.0; end
  def surface_iti;     @p.respond_to?(:surface_iti)     ? @p.surface_iti.to_f     : 25.5; end
  def surface_sarking; @p.respond_to?(:surface_sarking) ? @p.surface_sarking.to_f : 40.0; end
end

===== app/assets/fichiers_internes/aid_rules_seeds.rb =====

# ============================================================
# Règles d'aides — Grand Nancy + National
# Sources : ANAH, ALEC Nancy (échanges Fabien Collet / Thibaud Diehl)
# Dernière mise à jour : mars 2026
# ============================================================

AidRule.delete_all

# ============================================================
# 1. MaPrimeRénov' — Parcours accompagné
# ============================================================
AidRule.create!(
  name:         "MaPrimeRénov' — Parcours accompagné",
  slug:         "mpr_parcours_accompagne",
  aid_type:     "mpr",
  territory:    "national",
  description:  "Aide nationale pour rénovation globale. Nécessite un Accompagnateur Rénov', " \
                "au moins 2 gestes d'isolation, traitement de la ventilation, et saut minimum de 2 classes DPE.",
  conditions: {
    nb_gestes_isolation:    { operator: ">=", value: 2 },
    ventilation_traitee:    { operator: "==", value: true },
    dpe_saut_classes:       { operator: ">=", value: 2 },
    # Revenus : taux différents selon bracket
    income_bracket:         { operator: "in", value: ["tres_modeste", "modeste", "intermediaire", "superieur"] }
  },
  # Taux selon revenus (% du coût HT, plafonné)
  amount_type:  "percentage",
  amount_base:  "cost_ht",
  amount_notes: "Très modestes : 70% | Modestes : 50% | Intermédiaires : 35% | Supérieurs : 15% — " \
                "Bonus sortie passoire (+10%) si DPE G/F → D ou mieux. Plafond 70 000 € HT de travaux.",
  amount_max:   49000.0,  # 70% × 70 000
  amount_min:   nil,
  valid_from:   Date.new(2024, 1, 1),
  valid_until:  nil,
  active:       true,
  source_url:   "https://www.maprimerenov.gouv.fr",
  source_label: "ANAH — MaPrimeRénov' 2024",
  priority:     10
)

# ============================================================
# 2. CEE — Isolation thermique (certificats d'économie d'énergie)
# ============================================================
AidRule.create!(
  name:         "CEE — Isolation thermique",
  slug:         "cee_isolation",
  aid_type:     "cee",
  territory:    "national",
  description:  "Primes CEE via artisan RGE. Montants indicatifs basés sur les retours terrain ALEC Nancy " \
                "(Thibaud Diehl, juin 2025). Variables selon les offres des fournisseurs d'énergie.",
  conditions: {
    artisan_rge:            { operator: "==", value: true }
  },
  amount_type:  "fixed",
  amount_notes: "ITE : ~565 € | ITI : ~320 € | VMC double flux : 250–300 € | Sarking : ~533 € " \
                "(source : ALEC Nancy, devis Rénov-Habitat 54, juin 2025)",
  amount_value: nil,   # calculé poste par poste dans le service
  amount_max:   nil,
  valid_from:   Date.new(2024, 1, 1),
  valid_until:  nil,
  active:       true,
  source_url:   "https://www.ecologie.gouv.fr/dispositif-des-certificats-deconomies-denergie",
  source_label: "ADEME — CEE 2024 + retours terrain ALEC Nancy",
  priority:     20
)

# ============================================================
# 3. Éco-PTZ — Bouquet de travaux
# ============================================================
AidRule.create!(
  name:         "Éco-PTZ — Prêt à taux zéro travaux",
  slug:         "eco_ptz",
  aid_type:     "eco_ptz",
  territory:    "national",
  description:  "Prêt à taux zéro sans condition de revenus. Remboursable lors de la vente du bien. " \
                "30 000 € max pour 3 gestes ou plus (bouquet). 50 000 € en parcours accompagné.",
  conditions: {
    nb_gestes:              { operator: ">=", value: 3 }
  },
  amount_type:  "fixed",
  amount_value: 30000.0,
  amount_max:   50000.0,  # parcours accompagné
  amount_min:   nil,
  amount_base:  nil,
  amount_notes: "30 000 € pour bouquet 3 gestes. 50 000 € si parcours accompagné. " \
                "Durée max 15 ans. Soldé à la vente du bien (source : ALEC Nancy, mars 2025).",
  valid_from:   Date.new(2024, 1, 1),
  valid_until:  nil,
  active:       true,
  source_url:   "https://www.service-public.fr/particuliers/vosdroits/F19905",
  source_label: "Service Public — Éco-PTZ",
  priority:     30
)

# ============================================================
# 4. Grand Nancy — Prime "dernier geste" isolation
# ============================================================
AidRule.create!(
  name:         "Grand Nancy — Prime dernier geste isolation",
  slug:         "grand_nancy_dernier_geste",
  aid_type:     "local",
  territory:    "grand_nancy",
  description:  "Prime de la Métropole du Grand Nancy pour le dernier geste d'isolation manquant, " \
                "dans le cadre du PCAET. Nécessite un audit/DPE prouvant que c'est le dernier geste " \
                "ET que les travaux permettent d'atteindre au minimum l'étiquette C. " \
                "Justifier pourquoi le plancher bas n'est pas isolé (dalle sur terre-plein).",
  conditions: {
    territory:              { operator: "==", value: "grand_nancy" },
    dpe_cible:              { operator: "in", value: ["A", "B", "C"] },
    dernier_geste:          { operator: "==", value: true },
    audit_energetique:      { operator: "==", value: true }
  },
  amount_type:  "per_m2",
  amount_notes: "ITE : 30 €/m² | ITI : 10 €/m² | Sarking (toiture extérieure) : 50 €/m² " \
                "(source : ALEC Nancy, Thibaud Diehl, juin 2025 — PCAET Métropole Grand Nancy)",
  amount_value: nil,   # calculé selon le poste
  amount_max:   nil,
  valid_from:   Date.new(2024, 1, 1),
  valid_until:  nil,
  active:       true,
  source_url:   "https://www.grandnancy.eu/vivre-habiter/primes-energie/particuliers",
  source_label: "Métropole du Grand Nancy — PCAET",
  priority:     40
)

# ============================================================
# 5. Grand Nancy — Bonus BBC (rénovation globale A/B)
# ============================================================
AidRule.create!(
  name:         "Grand Nancy — Bonus BBC rénovation globale",
  slug:         "grand_nancy_bonus_bbc",
  aid_type:     "local",
  territory:    "grand_nancy",
  description:  "Bonification de la Métropole du Grand Nancy pour atteinte d'une étiquette A ou B " \
                "dans le cadre du parcours de rénovation globale. Majore le taux MPR de 5%. " \
                "N'est pas cumulable avec la prime dernier geste.",
  conditions: {
    territory:              { operator: "==", value: "grand_nancy" },
    dpe_cible:              { operator: "in", value: ["A", "B"] },
    parcours_accompagne:    { operator: "==", value: true }
  },
  amount_type:  "percentage",
  amount_base:  "cost_ht",
  amount_notes: "Majoration de 5% du taux MPR. Exemple : intermédiaires 35% + 5% = 40% " \
                "sur un coût HT de 50 000 € → aide totale ~20 000 € (source : ALEC Nancy, avril 2025).",
  amount_value: 5.0,    # +5% sur le taux MPR
  amount_max:   nil,
  valid_from:   Date.new(2024, 1, 1),
  valid_until:  nil,
  active:       true,
  source_url:   "https://www.grandnancy.eu/vivre-habiter/primes-energie/particuliers",
  source_label: "Métropole du Grand Nancy — Bonus BBC",
  priority:     50
)

puts "✅ #{AidRule.count} règles d'aides créées"
puts ""
AidRule.order(:priority).each do |r|
  puts "  #{r.priority.to_s.rjust(2)}. [#{r.territory.upcase}] #{r.name}"
end

<div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-8">

        Au lieu de 20 %. S'applique directement sur le devis. L'installation et la fourniture doivent figurer
        sur une seule facture, réalisée par un électricien qualifié IRVE.
      </p>

    <%# Copropriété %>
    <div class="bg-white border border-gray-100 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm">

        <div>
          <p class="font-bold text-gray-900">Infrastructure collective en copropriété</p>
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Prime réservée aux syndicats de copropriétaires</p>
        </div>
        <span class="text-sm font-bold text-purple-700 bg-purple-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full shrink-0">jusqu'à 8 000 €</span>
      </div>
      <p class="text-sm text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
        Si votre copropriété vote en AG la création d'une infrastructure collective,
        la prime ADVENIR peut atteindre 8 000 € pour le réseau commun,
        + 1 660 € par point de recharge partagé.
      </p>
      <p class="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-2">Bâtiments construits avant 2017. Doit être voté en AG avant demande de prime.</p>
    </div>

  </div>

      <li class="flex items-start gap-3"><span class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5">1</span> <span>Vérifiez votre place de parking et l'accès au tableau électrique.</span></li>
      <li class="flex items-start gap-3"><span class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5">2</span> <span>Contactez un installateur <strong>qualifié IRVE</strong> labellisé ADVENIR — il gère les démarches de prime.</span></li>
      <li class="flex items-start gap-3"><span class="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5">3</span> <span>Si vous êtes locataire : envoyez votre demande au syndic par <strong>LRAR</strong> avant de commander.</span></li>

class CreateAidRules < ActiveRecord::Migration[7.2]
  def change
    create_table :aid_rules do |t|
      t.string   :name,            null: false
      t.string   :slug,            null: false, index: { unique: true }
      t.string   :aid_type        # mpr, cee, eco_ptz, local
      t.string   :territory       # national, grand_nancy
      t.text     :description

      # Conditions d'éligibilité (JSON)
      t.jsonb    :conditions,      default: {}

      # Calcul du montant
      t.string   :amount_type     # fixed, per_m2, percentage, formula
      t.decimal  :amount_value,   precision: 10, scale: 2
      t.decimal  :amount_max,     precision: 10, scale: 2
      t.decimal  :amount_min,     precision: 10, scale: 2
      t.string   :amount_base     # cost_ht, cost_ttc, surface_m2
      t.text     :amount_notes    # explications lisibles

      # Validité
      t.date     :valid_from,      null: false
      t.date     :valid_until
      t.boolean  :active,          default: true

      # Traçabilité
      t.string   :source_url
      t.string   :source_label
      t.integer  :priority,        default: 0  # ordre d'application

      t.timestamps
    end

    # Index pour la recherche par territoire et type
    add_index :aid_rules, [:territory, :aid_type, :active]
  end
end

===== app/assets/fichiers_internes/properties_controller.rb =====

class PropertiesController < ApplicationController
  before_action :authenticate_user!, except: [:index, :new, :create, :show]
  before_action :set_property, only: [:show, :edit, :update, :destroy, :analyze, :publish, :unpublish, :preview]

  def index
    if user_signed_in?
      @properties = current_user.properties.order(created_at: :desc)
      render :index
    else
      redirect_to root_path
    end
  end

  def show
    if user_signed_in? && current_user == @property.user
      @aid_result = AidCalculatorService.new(@property).call
    end
  end

  def new
    @property = Property.new
  end

  def create
    if user_signed_in?
      @property = current_user.properties.build(property_params)
      @property.status = :analyzing
      if @property.save
        attach_uploaded_documents
        run_analysis(@property)
        redirect_to @property, notice: "Analyse complète générée."
      else
        render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
      end
    else
      session[:pending_property] = property_params.to_h
      redirect_to new_user_registration_path, notice: "Créez votre compte pour sauvegarder votre dossier."
    end
  end

  def edit
  end

  def update
    if @property.update(property_params)
      redirect_to @property, notice: "Bien mis à jour."
    else
      render :edit, status: :unprocessable_entity
    end
  end

  def destroy
    @property.destroy
    redirect_to properties_path, notice: "Bien supprimé."
  end

  def analyze
    run_analysis(@property)
    redirect_to @property, notice: "Analyse relancée avec succès."
  end

  def publish
    @property.update(status: :published)
    redirect_to @property, notice: "Votre dossier est maintenant visible par les prestataires."
  end

  def unpublish
    @property.update(status: :analyzed)
    redirect_to @property, notice: "Votre bien a été dépublié."
  end

  def preview
  end

  def update_dpe_target
    @property.update(dpe_target: params[:dpe_target])
    redirect_to @property, notice: "Objectif DPE mis à jour."
  end

  private

  def run_analysis(property)
    property.update(status: :analyzing)
    property.documents.each do |doc|
      DocumentAnalysisService.new(doc).call if doc.file.attached?
    end
    PropertyDataExtractorService.new(property).call
    DvfEstimationService.new(property).call
    DeviceSimulationService.new(property).call
    PropertyAnalysisService.new(property).call
    LocalAidCalculator.new(property).call
    property.update(status: :analyzed)
  end

  def attach_uploaded_documents
    uploaded = params.dig(:property, :uploaded_files)
    return unless uploaded.present?

    Array(uploaded).each do |file|
      next unless file.respond_to?(:original_filename)

      doc_type = detect_document_type(file.original_filename)
      doc = @property.documents.build(
        document_type: doc_type,
        name: file.original_filename
      )
      doc.file.attach(file)
      doc.save
    end
  end

  def detect_document_type(filename)
    name = filename.downcase
    return :dpe             if name.include?("dpe")
    return :pv_ag           if name.include?("pv") || name.include?("coprop")
    return :titre_propriete if name.include?("attestation") || name.include?("acte") || name.include?("vente") || name.include?("aae")
    return :photo           if name.match?(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/)
    :autre
  end

  def set_property
    if user_signed_in? && current_user.properties.exists?(params[:id])
      @property = current_user.properties.find(params[:id])
    else
      @property = Property.published.find(params[:id])
    end
  end

  def property_params
    params.require(:property).permit(
      :address, :city, :zipcode, :surface, :property_type,
      :construction_year, :dpe_class, :nb_rooms, :nb_lots,
      :is_copropriete, :description, :vacant, :source,
      :vacancy_duration, :vacancy_reason, :dpe_target, :income_bracket
    )
  end
end

===== app/assets/fichiers_internes/property_data_extractor_service(2).rb =====

class PropertyDataExtractorService
  ANTHROPIC_API_URL = "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages"

  def initialize(property)
    @property = property
  end

  def call
    summaries = collect_document_texts

    if summaries.blank?
      sync_from_existing_analysis
      return false
    end

    extracted = extract_structured_data(summaries)
    update_property(extracted)
    true
  end

  private

  def collect_document_texts
    texts = []

    @property.documents.reload.each do |doc|
      next unless doc.file.attached?

      # Si pas encore de résumé IA, tenter de l'analyser maintenant
      if doc.ai_summary.blank?
        begin
          DocumentAnalysisService.new(doc).call
          doc.reload
        rescue => e
          Rails.logger.error("PropertyDataExtractor: DocumentAnalysisService failed for doc #{doc.id}: #{e.message}")
        end
      end

      if doc.ai_summary.present?
        texts << "=== #{doc.document_type.humanize} ===\n#{doc.ai_summary}"
      else
        raw = extract_pdf_text(doc)
        texts << "=== #{doc.document_type.humanize} ===\n#{raw.truncate(3000)}" if raw.present?
      end
    end

    texts.join("\n\n")
  end

  def extract_pdf_text(document)
    Tempfile.create(["doc", ".pdf"]) do |tmp|
      tmp.binmode
      tmp.write(document.file.download)
      tmp.rewind
      reader = PDF::Reader.new(tmp.path)
      reader.pages.map(&:text).join("\n")
    end
  rescue => e
    Rails.logger.error("PDF extraction error: #{e.message}")
    ""
  end

  def extract_structured_data(content)
    prompt = <<~PROMPT
      Tu es un expert immobilier français. Analyse ces documents et extrais les données structurées du bien immobilier.

      Documents :
      ---
      #{content.truncate(10000)}
      ---

      Réponds UNIQUEMENT avec un objet JSON valide (sans markdown, sans commentaires) contenant ces champs :
      {
        "surface": <nombre entier en m², null si inconnu>,
        "construction_year": <année entière pour les calculs. Si "avant XXXX" → XXXX. Si "années 1970" → 1970. null si inconnu>,
        "construction_period": <texte exact tel qu'indiqué dans le document, ex: "Avant 1948", "1975", "Entre 1948 et 1974". null si inconnu>,
        "dpe_class": <"A","B","C","D","E","F" ou "G", null si inconnu>,
        "dpe_value": <valeur kWhEP/m².an en entier, null si inconnue>,
        "property_type": <"appartement" ou "maison", null si inconnu>,
        "nb_rooms": <nombre entier de pièces, null si inconnu>,
        "nb_lots": <nombre entier de lots en copropriété, null si inconnu>,
        "is_copropriete": <true ou false>,
        "purchase_price": <prix d'achat en euros entier, null si inconnu>,
        "heating_system": <description courte du système de chauffage, null si inconnu>,
        "wall_insulation": <description de l'isolation des murs, null si inconnue>,
        "roof_insulation": <description de l'isolation de la toiture, null si inconnue>
      }

      Priorité aux données officielles (DPE, acte notarié, certificat Carrez).
      Ne mets jamais de valeur inventée — préfère null.
    PROMPT

    response = call_claude(prompt)
    clean = response.to_s.strip.gsub(/\A```(?:json)?\s*/i, "").gsub(/\s*```\z/, "").strip
    JSON.parse(clean)
  rescue JSON::ParserError => e
    Rails.logger.error("PropertyDataExtractor JSON parse error: #{e.message}\nRaw: #{response}")
    {}
  end

  def update_property(data)
    return if data.blank?

    updates = {}

    updates[:surface]             = data["surface"]             if @property.surface.blank?             && data["surface"].present?
    updates[:construction_year]   = data["construction_year"]   if @property.construction_year.blank?   && data["construction_year"].present?
    updates[:construction_period] = data["construction_period"] if @property.construction_period.blank? && data["construction_period"].present?
    updates[:dpe_class]           = data["dpe_class"]           if @property.dpe_class.blank?           && data["dpe_class"].present?
    updates[:nb_rooms]            = data["nb_rooms"]            if @property.nb_rooms.blank?            && data["nb_rooms"].present?
    updates[:nb_lots]             = data["nb_lots"]             if @property.nb_lots.blank?             && data["nb_lots"].present?

    if @property.property_type.blank? && data["property_type"].present?
      type = data["property_type"].downcase
      updates[:property_type] = type if Property.property_types.key?(type)
    end

    updates[:is_copropriete] = true if data["is_copropriete"] == true

    if @property.description.blank?
      extras = []
      extras << "Chauffage : #{data['heating_system']}"          if data["heating_system"].present?
      extras << "Isolation murs : #{data['wall_insulation']}"    if data["wall_insulation"].present?
      extras << "Isolation toiture : #{data['roof_insulation']}" if data["roof_insulation"].present?
      extras << "Prix d'acquisition : #{data['purchase_price']} €" if data["purchase_price"].present?
      updates[:description] = extras.join(" | ") if extras.any?
    end

    if updates.any?
      @property.update(updates)
      Rails.logger.info("PropertyDataExtractor updated: #{updates.keys.join(', ')}")
    end
  end

  # Fallback : si aucun document n'a d'ai_summary,
  # récupère le profil_extrait depuis l'analyse IA existante
  def sync_from_existing_analysis
    return unless @property.analysis&.content.present?
    json = JSON.parse(@property.analysis.content) rescue nil
    return unless json
    profil = json["profil_extrait"] || {}
    update_property(profil.transform_keys(&:to_s))
  end

  def call_claude(prompt)
    response = HTTParty.post(
      ANTHROPIC_API_URL,
      headers: {
        "Content-Type"      => "application/json",
        "x-api-key"         => ENV["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"],
        "anthropic-version" => "2023-06-01"
      },
      body: {
        model:      "claude-sonnet-4-6",
        max_tokens: 512,
        messages:   [{ role: "user", content: prompt }]
      }.to_json
    )
    JSON.parse(response.body).dig("content", 0, "text")
  end
end

          <script>
          function calcTravaux() {
            var cbs = document.querySelectorAll('.travaux-cb');
            var min = 0, max = 0, gains = [];
            cbs.forEach(function(cb) {
              if (cb.checked) {
                min += parseInt(cb.dataset.min) || 0;
                max += parseInt(cb.dataset.max) || 0;
                if (cb.dataset.gain) gains.push(cb.dataset.gain);
              }
            });
            var fmt = function(x){ return x.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €'; };
            document.getElementById('travaux-montant').textContent = fmt(min) + ' – ' + fmt(max);
            document.getElementById('travaux-dpe-impact').textContent = gains.length > 0 ? 'Impact estimé : ' + gains.join(', ') : 'Aucun poste sélectionné';
            document.getElementById('travaux-total').style.background = gains.length > 0 ? '#111827' : '#6b7280';
            var simSlider = document.getElementById('sim-montant');
            if (simSlider && max > 0) { simSlider.value = max; if (typeof calcPret === 'function') calcPret(); }
            if (typeof calcAides === 'function') calcAides();
          }
          document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', calcTravaux);
          </script>
        <% end %>

    <script>
    function calcPret() {
      var M = parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-montant').value);
      var D = parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-duree').value);
      var T = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-taux').value);
      document.getElementById('sim-montant-label').textContent = M.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €';
      document.getElementById('sim-duree-label').textContent = D + ' ans';
      document.getElementById('sim-taux-label').textContent = T.toFixed(1) + ' %';
      var n = D * 12;
      var fmt = function(x){ return Math.round(x).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €'; };
      if (T === 0) {
        var mens = M / n;
        document.getElementById('sim-mensualite').textContent = fmt(mens) + '/mois';
        document.getElementById('sim-cout-total').textContent = fmt(M);
        document.getElementById('sim-interets').textContent = '0 €';
      } else {
        var r = (T / 100) / 12;
        var mens = M * r * Math.pow(1+r,n) / (Math.pow(1+r,n)-1);
        var total = mens * n;
        document.getElementById('sim-mensualite').textContent = fmt(mens) + '/mois';
        document.getElementById('sim-cout-total').textContent = fmt(total);
        document.getElementById('sim-interets').textContent = fmt(total - M);
      }
    }
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', calcPret);

var currentProfil = -1;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', calcPret);
var currentProfil = -1;
var mprTaux = [0.70, 0.50, 0.35, 0.15];
var mprLabels = ['Bleu - 70% max', 'Jaune - 50% max', 'Violet - 35% max', 'Rose - 15% max'];
function setProfil(p) {
  currentProfil = p;
  document.querySelectorAll('.profil-btn').forEach(function(b, i) {
    b.style.borderColor = i === p ? '#059669' : '';
    b.style.color = i === p ? '#047857' : '';
    b.style.background = i === p ? '#ecfdf5' : '';
  });
  calcAides();
}
function calcAides() {
  var cbs = document.querySelectorAll('.travaux-cb');
  var maxBudget = 0;
  cbs.forEach(function(cb) { if (cb.checked) maxBudget += parseInt(cb.dataset.max) || 0; });
  var cee = maxBudget > 0 ? Math.min(Math.round(maxBudget * 0.08), 4000) : 0;
  document.getElementById('aide-cee').textContent = cee > 0 ? cee.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €' : '—';
  var mpr = 0;
  if (currentProfil >= 0 && maxBudget > 0) {
    mpr = Math.min(Math.round(maxBudget * mprTaux[currentProfil]), 20000);
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr').textContent = mpr.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €';
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr-label').textContent = mprLabels[currentProfil];
  } else {
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr').textContent = '—';
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr-label').textContent = 'Sélectionnez votre profil';
  }
  var totalAides = (currentProfil >= 0 ? mpr : 0) + cee;
  var reste = Math.max(0, maxBudget - totalAides);
  document.getElementById('reste-financer').textContent = reste > 0 ? reste.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €' : maxBudget > 0 ? '0 €' : '—';
  var slider = document.getElementById('sim-montant');
  if (slider && reste > 0) { slider.value = reste; calcPret(); }
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() { calcTravaux(); calcPret(); calcAides(); });
    </script>

</div>

    <%# Panneau 2 : Simulateur bancaire %>
    <div style="background:white;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:1.5rem;padding:1.5rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;">
      <div style="font-size:2rem;margin-bottom:12px;">🧮</div>

      </p>
      <button onclick="toggleSimulation()"
              style="display:block;width:100%;background:#059669;color:white;padding:12px;border-radius:12px;border:none;font-weight:700;font-size:0.875rem;cursor:pointer;margin-top:auto;">
        🧮 Lancer la simulation
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <%# Formulaire simulateur (masqué par défaut) %>
  <div id="simulation-bancaire" style="display:none;background:white;border:2px solid #059669;border-radius:1.5rem;padding:2rem;margin-bottom:1.5rem;">
    <h3 style="font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:800;color:#111827;margin:0 0 4px;">🧮 Simulation de financement personnalisée</h3>
    <p style="font-size:0.8rem;color:#6b7280;margin:0 0 1.5rem;">Ces informations restent sur votre appareil et ne sont pas transmises à Lotys.</p>
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:1rem;margin-bottom:1rem;">
      <div>
        <label style="display:block;font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:#374151;margin-bottom:4px;">Revenus nets mensuels</label>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;">
          <input type="number" id="sim-revenus" placeholder="ex : 2 800" min="0" style="flex:1;padding:10px 12px;border:none;outline:none;font-size:0.875rem;" oninput="calculerSimulation()">
          <span style="padding:10px 12px;background:#f9fafb;color:#9ca3af;font-size:0.8rem;">€/mois</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <label style="display:block;font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:#374151;margin-bottom:4px;">Crédits en cours</label>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;">
          <input type="number" id="sim-charges" placeholder="ex : 400" min="0" value="0" style="flex:1;padding:10px 12px;border:none;outline:none;font-size:0.875rem;" oninput="calculerSimulation()">
          <span style="padding:10px 12px;background:#f9fafb;color:#9ca3af;font-size:0.8rem;">€/mois</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <label style="display:block;font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:#374151;margin-bottom:4px;">Apport disponible</label>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;">
          <input type="number" id="sim-apport" placeholder="ex : 5 000" min="0" value="0" style="flex:1;padding:10px 12px;border:none;outline:none;font-size:0.875rem;" oninput="calculerSimulation()">
          <span style="padding:10px 12px;background:#f9fafb;color:#9ca3af;font-size:0.8rem;">€</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div>
        <label style="display:block;font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:#374151;margin-bottom:4px;">Votre âge</label>
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;">
          <input type="number" id="sim-age" placeholder="ex : 45" min="18" max="80" style="flex:1;padding:10px 12px;border:none;outline:none;font-size:0.875rem;" oninput="calculerSimulation()">
          <span style="padding:10px 12px;background:#f9fafb;color:#9ca3af;font-size:0.8rem;">ans</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="margin-bottom:1rem;">
      <label style="display:block;font-size:0.8rem;font-weight:600;color:#374151;margin-bottom:4px;">Situation professionnelle</label>
      <select id="sim-situation" onchange="calculerSimulation()" style="width:100%;padding:10px 12px;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;font-size:0.875rem;background:white;">
        <option value="cdi">CDI / Fonctionnaire</option>
        <option value="independant">Indépendant / Professions libérales</option>
        <option value="retraite">Retraité</option>
      </select>
    </div>
    <div id="sim-resultats" style="display:none;background:#f0fdf4;border:1px solid #bbf7d0;border-radius:1rem;padding:1.5rem;margin-top:1rem;">
      <h4 style="font-size:0.875rem;font-weight:700;color:#166534;margin:0 0 1rem;">📊 Résultats de votre simulation</h4>
      <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:1rem;">
        <div style="background:white;border-radius:0.75rem;padding:1rem;text-align:center;">
          <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b7280;margin:0 0 4px;">Mensualité estimée</p>
          <p id="res-mensualite" style="font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:900;color:#059669;margin:0;">—</p>
        </div>
        <div style="background:white;border-radius:0.75rem;padding:1rem;text-align:center;">
          <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b7280;margin:0 0 4px;">Taux d'endettement</p>
          <p id="res-endettement" style="font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:900;margin:0;">—</p>
          <p id="res-endettement-label" style="font-size:0.65rem;margin:4px 0 0;">—</p>
        </div>
        <div style="background:white;border-radius:0.75rem;padding:1rem;text-align:center;">
          <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b7280;margin:0 0 4px;">Durée recommandée</p>
          <p id="res-duree" style="font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:900;color:#1e40af;margin:0;">—</p>
        </div>
        <div style="background:white;border-radius:0.75rem;padding:1rem;text-align:center;">
          <p style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#6b7280;margin:0 0 4px;">Coût total du crédit</p>
          <p id="res-cout" style="font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:900;color:#374151;margin:0;">—</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="res-alerte" style="display:none;margin-top:1rem;padding:12px;border-radius:0.75rem;font-size:0.8rem;"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    const RESTE_FINANCER = <%= reste_a_charge %>;

    function toggleSimulation() {
      const el = document.getElementById('simulation-bancaire');
      el.style.display = el.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none';
      if (el.style.display === 'block') el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
    }

    function formatEur(n) {
      return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR', { style: 'currency', currency: 'EUR', maximumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(n);
    }

    function mensualite(capital, tauxAnnuel, mois) {
      if (tauxAnnuel === 0) return capital / mois;
      const t = tauxAnnuel / 12;
      return capital * t / (1 - Math.pow(1 + t, -mois));
    }

    function calculerSimulation() {
      const revenus   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-revenus').value) || 0;
      const charges   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-charges').value) || 0;
      const apport    = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-apport').value) || 0;
      const age       = parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-age').value) || 0;
      const situation = document.getElementById('sim-situation').value;

      if (revenus < 500 || age < 18) return;

      const dureeMax  = Math.min(Math.max(75 - age, 5), 20);
      const dureeReco = Math.min(dureeMax, 15);
      const taux = situation === 'cdi' ? 0.033 : situation === 'independant' ? 0.037 : 0.038;

      const capitalPret = Math.max(RESTE_FINANCER - apport, 0);
      const mensTotal   = capitalPret > 0 ? mensualite(capitalPret, taux, dureeReco * 12) : 0;
      const tauxEndet   = ((charges + mensTotal) / revenus) * 100;
      const coutTotal   = mensTotal * dureeReco * 12 - capitalPret;

      document.getElementById('sim-resultats').style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('res-mensualite').textContent = formatEur(mensTotal) + '/mois';
      document.getElementById('res-duree').textContent = dureeReco + ' ans';
      document.getElementById('res-cout').textContent = coutTotal > 0 ? formatEur(coutTotal) : '0 €';

      const endetEl = document.getElementById('res-endettement');
      const endetLabel = document.getElementById('res-endettement-label');
      endetEl.textContent = tauxEndet.toFixed(1) + '%';

      if (tauxEndet <= 33) {
        endetEl.style.color = '#059669';
        endetLabel.textContent = '✓ Dans les normes bancaires';
        endetLabel.style.color = '#059669';
      } else if (tauxEndet <= 40) {
        endetEl.style.color = '#d97706';
        endetLabel.textContent = '⚠ Limite acceptable';
        endetLabel.style.color = '#d97706';
      } else {
        endetEl.style.color = '#dc2626';
        endetLabel.textContent = '✗ Au-dessus des normes';
        endetLabel.style.color = '#dc2626';
      }

      const alerte = document.getElementById('res-alerte');
      if (tauxEndet > 40) {
        alerte.style.display = 'block';
        alerte.style.background = '#fef2f2';
        alerte.style.border = '1px solid #fecaca';
        alerte.style.color = '#991b1b';
        alerte.innerHTML = "⚠️ Votre taux d'endettement dépasse 40%. Un courtier peut parfois trouver des solutions adaptées.";
      } else if (capitalPret === 0) {
        alerte.style.display = 'block';
        alerte.style.background = '#f0fdf4';
        alerte.style.border = '1px solid #bbf7d0';
        alerte.style.color = '#166534';
        alerte.innerHTML = '🎉 Votre apport couvre l\'intégralité du reste à charge. Aucun emprunt complémentaire nécessaire.';
      } else {
        alerte.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }
  </script>

<% end %><%# /owner-only: financement %>

</div>

        <%= @property.draft? ? 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600' :
            @property.analyzing? ? 'bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700' :
            @property.analyzed? ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' :
            'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' %>">
        <%= @property.draft? ? '⏳ Brouillon' :
            @property.analyzing? ? '🔄 Analyse en cours' :
            @property.analyzed? ? '✅ Analysé' : '📢 Publié' %>
      </span>
    </div>
    <p class="text-gray-500"><%= @property.city %> <%= @property.zipcode %></p>
  </div>
  <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 sm:mt-0">
    <% if user_signed_in? && current_user == @property.user %>
    <%= link_to "Modifier", edit_property_path(@property), class: "text-center border border-gray-200 text-gray-600 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm hover:bg-gray-50 w-full sm:w-auto" %>
    <% if @property.draft? || @property.analyzed? %>
      <%= button_to "🔍 Lancer l'analyse IA", analyze_property_path(@property),
          class: "bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm hover:bg-blue-700 font-medium w-full sm:w-auto" %>
    <% end %>
    <% if @property.analyzed? %>
      <%= link_to "👁️ Prévisualiser et publier", preview_property_path(@property),
          class: "bg-emerald-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm hover:bg-emerald-700 font-medium w-full sm:w-auto text-center" %>
    <% end %>
    <% if @property.published? %>

          <script>
          function calcTravaux() {
            var cbs = document.querySelectorAll('.travaux-cb');
            var min = 0, max = 0;
            var gains = [];
            cbs.forEach(function(cb) {
              if (cb.checked) {
                min += parseInt(cb.dataset.min) || 0;
                max += parseInt(cb.dataset.max) || 0;
                if (cb.dataset.gain) gains.push(cb.dataset.gain);
              }
            });
            var fmt = function(x){ return x.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €'; };
            document.getElementById('travaux-montant').textContent = fmt(min) + ' – ' + fmt(max);
            document.getElementById('travaux-dpe-impact').textContent = gains.length > 0
              ? 'Impact : ' + gains.join(', ')
              : 'Aucun travaux sélectionné';
            var total = document.getElementById('travaux-total');
            total.style.background = gains.length > 0 ? '#047857' : '#6b7280';
            var simSlider = document.getElementById('sim-montant');
            if (simSlider && max > 0) { simSlider.value = max; if (typeof calcPret === 'function') calcPret(); }
            if (typeof calcAides === 'function') calcAides();
          }
          document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', calcTravaux);
          </script>
        <% end %>

    <script>
    function calcPret() {
      var M = parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-montant').value);
      var D = parseInt(document.getElementById('sim-duree').value);
      var T = parseFloat(document.getElementById('sim-taux').value);
      document.getElementById('sim-montant-label').textContent = M.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €';
      document.getElementById('sim-duree-label').textContent = D + ' ans';
      document.getElementById('sim-taux-label').textContent = T.toFixed(1) + ' %';
      var n = D * 12;
      var fmt = function(x){ return Math.round(x).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €'; };
      if (T === 0) {
        var mens = M / n;
        document.getElementById('sim-mensualite').textContent = fmt(mens) + '/mois';
        document.getElementById('sim-cout-total').textContent = fmt(M);
        document.getElementById('sim-interets').textContent = '0 €';
      } else {
        var r = (T / 100) / 12;
        var mens = M * r * Math.pow(1+r,n) / (Math.pow(1+r,n)-1);
        var total = mens * n;
        document.getElementById('sim-mensualite').textContent = fmt(mens) + '/mois';
        document.getElementById('sim-cout-total').textContent = fmt(total);
        document.getElementById('sim-interets').textContent = fmt(total - M);
      }
    }
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', calcPret);

var currentProfil = -1;
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', calcPret);
var currentProfil = -1;
var mprTaux = [0.70, 0.50, 0.35, 0.15];
var mprLabels = ['Bleu - 70% max', 'Jaune - 50% max', 'Violet - 35% max', 'Rose - 15% max'];
function setProfil(p) {
  currentProfil = p;
  document.querySelectorAll('.profil-btn').forEach(function(b, i) {
    b.style.borderColor = i === p ? '#059669' : '';
    b.style.color = i === p ? '#047857' : '';
    b.style.background = i === p ? '#ecfdf5' : '';
  });
  calcAides();
}
function calcAides() {
  var cbs = document.querySelectorAll('.travaux-cb');
  var maxBudget = 0;
  cbs.forEach(function(cb) { if (cb.checked) maxBudget += parseInt(cb.dataset.max) || 0; });
  var cee = maxBudget > 0 ? Math.min(Math.round(maxBudget * 0.08), 4000) : 0;
  document.getElementById('aide-cee').textContent = cee > 0 ? cee.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €' : '—';
  var mpr = 0;
  if (currentProfil >= 0 && maxBudget > 0) {
    mpr = Math.min(Math.round(maxBudget * mprTaux[currentProfil]), 20000);
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr').textContent = mpr.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €';
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr-label').textContent = mprLabels[currentProfil];
  } else {
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr').textContent = '—';
    document.getElementById('aide-mpr-label').textContent = 'Sélectionnez votre profil';
  }
  var totalAides = (currentProfil >= 0 ? mpr : 0) + cee;
  var reste = Math.max(0, maxBudget - totalAides);
  document.getElementById('reste-financer').textContent = reste > 0 ? reste.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' €' : maxBudget > 0 ? '0 €' : '—';
  var slider = document.getElementById('sim-montant');
  if (slider && reste > 0) { slider.value = reste; calcPret(); }
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() { calcTravaux(); calcPret(); calcAides(); });
    </script>
    <% end %>

    <% i = parsed&.[]("idees") %>

      <div class="flex items-start gap-3 sm:gap-4 px-4 sm:px-6 pt-4 sm:pt-6 pb-4 sm:pb-5 border-b-2 border-gray-100 bg-gray-50">
        <img src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1497366216548-37526070297c?w=200&q=80&auto=format&fit=crop" alt="" class="w-16 h-16 sm:w-24 sm:h-24 lg:w-28 lg:h-28 rounded-xl object-cover shadow-sm flex-shrink-0">

===== app/controllers/admin/local_aid_schemes_controller.rb =====

module Admin
  class LocalAidSchemesController < ApplicationController
    before_action :authenticate_user!
    before_action :require_admin!
    before_action :set_scheme, only: [:show, :edit, :update, :destroy, :toggle_active]

    def index
      @schemes = LocalAidScheme.order(:territory, :aid_type)
    end

    def show; end
    def new
      @scheme = LocalAidScheme.new(active: true)
    end

    def create
      @scheme = LocalAidScheme.new(scheme_params)
      if @scheme.save
        redirect_to admin_local_aid_schemes_path, notice: "Dispositif créé."
      else
        render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
      end
    end

    def edit; end

    def update
      if @scheme.update(scheme_params)
        redirect_to admin_local_aid_schemes_path, notice: "Dispositif mis à jour."
      else
        render :edit, status: :unprocessable_entity
      end
    end

    def destroy
      @scheme.destroy
      redirect_to admin_local_aid_schemes_path, notice: "Dispositif supprimé."
    end

    def toggle_active
      @scheme.update!(active: !@scheme.active)
      redirect_to admin_local_aid_schemes_path,
                  notice: "Dispositif #{@scheme.active? ? 'activé' : 'désactivé'}."
    end

    private

    def set_scheme
      @scheme = LocalAidScheme.find(params[:id])
    end

    def require_admin!
      redirect_to root_path, alert: "Accès non autorisé." unless current_user.admin?
    end

    def scheme_params
      params.require(:local_aid_scheme).permit(
        :name, :territory, :aid_type,
        :rate_tres_modeste, :rate_modeste, :rate_intermediaire, :rate_superieur,
        :max_tres_modeste, :max_modeste, :max_intermediaire, :max_superieur,
        :conditions_text, :warning_text,
        :contact_name, :contact_url, :source_url, :source_label,
        :valid_from, :valid_until, :active,
        zipcodes: [], property_types: []
      )
    end
  end
end

===== app/controllers/application_controller.rb =====

class ApplicationController < ActionController::Base
  layout :layout_by_resource

  def layout_by_resource
    devise_controller? ? "devise" : "application"
  end
  after_action :save_pending_property

  private

  def save_pending_property
    if user_signed_in? && session[:pending_property].present?
      current_user.properties.create(session.delete(:pending_property))
    end
  end
end

===== app/controllers/documents_controller.rb =====

class DocumentsController < ApplicationController
  before_action :authenticate_user!
  before_action :set_property

  def new
    @document = @property.documents.build
  end

  def create
    saved = 0
    Array(params.dig(:document, :files)).each do |file|
      next unless file.respond_to?(:original_filename)
      doc = @property.documents.build(
        document_type: params.dig(:document, :document_type),
        name: file.original_filename
      )
      doc.file.attach(file)
      doc.save!
      saved += 1
    end

    if saved == 0
      @document = @property.documents.build
      flash.now[:alert] = "Veuillez sélectionner au moins un fichier."
      render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity and return
    end

    Analysis.where(property: @property).delete_all
    @property.update!(status: :analyzing)
    PropertyAnalysisService.new(@property).call
    LocalAidCalculator.new(@property).call
    @property.update!(status: :analyzed)

    redirect_to @property, notice: "#{saved} document(s) ajouté(s) · Analyse IA relancée."
  rescue => e
    @document = @property.documents.build
    flash.now[:alert] = "Erreur : #{e.message}"
    render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
  end

  def destroy
    @document = @property.documents.find(params[:id])
    @document.destroy
    redirect_to property_path(@property, anchor: "documents"), notice: "Document supprimé."
  end

  private

  def set_property
    @property = current_user.properties.find(params[:property_id])
  end
end

===== app/controllers/offers_controller.rb =====

class OffersController < ApplicationController
  before_action :authenticate_user!, except: [:index, :new]

  def index
    if user_signed_in? && current_user.proprietaire?
      @offers = Offer.joins(:property)
                     .where(properties: { user_id: current_user.id })
                     .includes(:property, :user)
                     .order(created_at: :desc)
    end
    @properties = Property.published
                           .includes(:valuation, :device_simulation, :offers)
                           .order(created_at: :desc)
  end

  def create
    authenticate_user! and return unless user_signed_in?
    @property = Property.published.find(params[:property_id])
    @offer = @property.offers.build(offer_params)
    @offer.user = current_user
    @offer.status = :pending
    @offer.expires_at = 30.days.from_now
    if @offer.save
      redirect_to offers_path, notice: "Votre offre a été transmise au propriétaire."
    else
      render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
    end
  end

  def new
  @property = Property.published.find(params[:property_id])
  @offer = @property.offers.build
  end

  def update
    @offer = Offer.joins(:property)
                  .where(properties: { user_id: current_user.id })
                  .find(params[:id])
    @offer.update(status: params[:status])
    redirect_to offers_path, notice: "Offre mise à jour."
  end

  private

  def offer_params
    params.require(:offer).permit(:offer_type, :amount, :description)
  end
end

  end

===== app/controllers/properties_controller.rb =====

class PropertiesController < ApplicationController
  before_action :authenticate_user!, except: [:index, :new, :create, :show]
  before_action :set_property, only: [:show, :edit, :update, :destroy, :analyze, :publish, :unpublish, :preview]

  def index
    if user_signed_in?
      @properties = current_user.properties.order(created_at: :desc)
      render :index
    else
      redirect_to root_path
    end
  end

  def show
    if user_signed_in? && current_user == @property.user
      @aid_result = AidCalculatorService.new(@property).call
    end
  end

  def new
    @property = Property.new
  end

  def create
    if user_signed_in?
      @property = current_user.properties.build(property_params)
      @property.status = :analyzing
      if @property.save
        attach_uploaded_documents
        run_analysis(@property)
        redirect_to @property, notice: "Analyse complète générée."
      else
        render :new, status: :unprocessable_entity
      end
    else
      session[:pending_property] = property_params.to_h
      redirect_to new_user_registration_path, notice: "Créez votre compte pour sauvegarder votre dossier."
    end
  end

  def edit
  end

  def update
    if @property.update(property_params)
      redirect_to @property, notice: "Bien mis à jour."
    else
      render :edit, status: :unprocessable_entity
    end
  end

  def destroy
    @property.destroy
    redirect_to properties_path, notice: "Bien supprimé."
  end

  def analyze
    run_analysis(@property)
    redirect_to @property, notice: "Analyse relancée avec succès."
  end

  def publish
    @property.update(status: :published)
    redirect_to @property, notice: "Votre dossier est maintenant visible par les prestataires."
  end

  def unpublish
    @property.update(status: :analyzed)
    redirect_to @property, notice: "Votre bien a été dépublié."
  end

  def preview
  end

  def update_dpe_target
    @property.update(dpe_target: params[:dpe_target])
    redirect_to @property, notice: "Objectif DPE mis à jour."
  end

  private

  def run_analysis(property)
    property.update(status: :analyzing)
    property.documents.each do |doc|
      DocumentAnalysisService.new(doc).call if doc.file.attached?
    end
    PropertyDataExtractorService.new(property).call
    DvfEstimationService.new(property).call
    DeviceSimulationService.new(property).call
    PropertyAnalysisService.new(property).call
    LocalAidCalculator.new(property).call
    property.update(status: :analyzed)
  end

  def attach_uploaded_documents
    uploaded = params.dig(:property, :uploaded_files)
    return unless uploaded.present?

    Array(uploaded).each do |file|
      next unless file.respond_to?(:original_filename)

      doc_type = detect_document_type(file.original_filename)
      doc = @property.documents.build(
        document_type: doc_type,
        name: file.original_filename
      )
      doc.file.attach(file)
      doc.save
    end
  end

  def detect_document_type(filename)
    name = filename.downcase
    return :dpe             if name.include?("dpe")
    return :pv_ag           if name.include?("pv") || name.include?("coprop")
    return :titre_propriete if name.include?("attestation") || name.include?("acte") || name.include?("vente") || name.include?("aae")
    return :photo           if name.match?(/\.(jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/)
    :autre
  end

  def set_property
    if user_signed_in? && current_user.properties.exists?(params[:id])
      @property = current_user.properties.find(params[:id])
    else
      @property = Property.published.find(params[:id])
    end
  end

  def borne_electrique
  end

  def property_params
    params.require(:property).permit(
      :address, :city, :zipcode, :surface, :property_type,
      :construction_year, :dpe_class, :nb_rooms, :nb_lots,
      :is_copropriete, :description, :vacant, :source,
      :vacancy_duration, :vacancy_reason, :dpe_target, :income_bracket
    )
  end
end

===== app/helpers/application_helper.rb =====

module ApplicationHelper
end

  def render_markdown(text)
    return "" if text.blank?
    renderer = Redcarpet::Render::HTML.new(filter_html: true, hard_wrap: true)
    markdown = Redcarpet::Markdown.new(renderer, tables: true, fenced_code_blocks: true, no_intra_emphasis: true)
    markdown.render(text).html_safe
  end

  def sentences_to_lines(text)
    return "" if text.blank?
    text.gsub(/\. (?=[A-ZÀ-Ü])/, ".\n")
  end

  def status_badge_class(status)

    when "draft"      then "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600"
    when "analyzing"  then "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700"
    when "analyzed"   then "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700"
    when "published"  then "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700"
    else "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600"
    end
  end

class LocalAidResult < ApplicationRecord
  belongs_to :property
  belongs_to :local_aid_scheme

  scope :eligible, -> { where(eligible: true) }

  def total_max_amount
    amounts.values.compact.max
  end

  def amount_for(bracket)
    amounts[bracket.to_s]
  end
end

===== app/models/local_aid_scheme.rb =====

class LocalAidScheme < ApplicationRecord
  has_many :local_aid_results, dependent: :destroy

  validates :name,      presence: true
  validates :territory, presence: true
  validates :aid_type,  presence: true, inclusion: { in: %w[renov_globale isolation] }

  scope :active, -> { where(active: true) }
  scope :currently_valid, -> {
    active
      .where("valid_from IS NULL OR valid_from <= ?", Date.today)
      .where("valid_until IS NULL OR valid_until >= ?", Date.today)
  }

  def covers_zipcode?(zipcode)
    zipcodes.include?(zipcode.to_s)
  end

  def covers_property_type?(ptype)
    property_types.nil? || property_types.include?(ptype.to_s)
  end

  def eligible_for?(property)
    covers_zipcode?(property.zipcode) && covers_property_type?(property.property_type)
  end

  def amount_for(bracket)
    case bracket.to_sym
    when :tres_modeste   then max_tres_modeste
    when :modeste        then max_modeste
    when :intermediaire  then max_intermediaire
    when :superieur      then max_superieur
    end
  end

  def renov_globale? = aid_type == "renov_globale"
  def isolation?     = aid_type == "isolation"

  def aid_type_label
    renov_globale? ? "Rénovation globale" : "Isolation"
  end
end

class Property < ApplicationRecord
  belongs_to :user

  has_one :valuation, dependent: :destroy
  has_one :device_simulation, dependent: :destroy
  has_many :offers, dependent: :destroy
  has_many :local_aid_results, dependent: :destroy

  enum status: { draft: 0, analyzing: 1, analyzed: 2, published: 3 }
  enum property_type: { appartement: 0, maison: 1 }, _prefix: :kind
  def local_aids_total_max
    local_aid_results.eligible.sum { |r| r.total_max_amount.to_i }
